Custom optimization ideas:
A. "Antidiagonal projection": We want all c_k low. Given current a, identify high anti-diagonals. For each high k, we can reduce products by moving mass from positions on that anti-diagonal to positions not causing high? This is like local moves.

B. "LinearLY: solve for p that minimizes max K_k p^2 via Frank-Wolfe?" The function f(p)=max_k p^T K_k p. It's quasi-convex? K_k indefinite but p>=0. Could use second-order cone? Maybe use penalty method with exponential.

C. use "alternating direction method of multipliers" for constraints c_k <= M. For fixed M, feasibility: minimize sum (c_k-M)_+^2 or log-barrier. We can binary search M and use gradient descent to find p with all c_k<=M. This may focus on feasibility. The minimal M* gives score. We can use projected gradient with log barrier: minimize F(p)=sum exp(c_k/τ) + regularization. As τ small, enforces max. But nonconvex.

D. use "analytic continuation" from uniform: start at uniform (score2) and optimize decreasing τ? Maybe.

E. use "randomized coordinate descent": For each i, adjust a_i to reduce max. Since c_k quadratic, derivative simple. Could be efficient.

F. use "genetic" recombination of good sequences.

def conv_fft(a,b):
    na, nb = len(a), len(b)
    n = 1 << (na+nb-2).bit_length()
    fa = rfft(a,n)
    fb = rfft(b,n)
    c = irfft(fa*fb,n)[:na+nb-1]
    c += 0j? real; clip tiny negatives to 0.
    return c.real
```

For cyclic group of odd order, a starter is a set of (v-1)/2 pairs partitioning nonzero elements such that differences cover each nonzero. Pair sums? In cyclic group of order 2m+1, a "Skolem sequence" pairs with distinct sums. If we have a set A and its complement? Wait if B = n-1-A (reflection), A and B partition. Pair sums A+A? We can choose A such that the multiset of sums A+A is as flat as possible. This is like "Sidon" but with no reflected pairs.

Maybe use modular construction: Work in cyclic group Z_N with N = 2^k? Let choose A = x mod N for x in 0..n-1? If select A = all even positions? Then A∩reflection? For r variables, qconv = convolution of (1+r)/2. Objective max. Gradient wrt r similar to p but with anti-sym. We can do multiplicative updates on q then project to anti-sym box? Projection: given q∈R^n, find r=2q-1 anti-sym and |r|≤1. Anti-sym projection: for each pair (i,j), average? We want r_i=-r_j and minimize (r_i-(2q_i-1))^2+(r_j-(2q_j-1))^2. Set s = ((2q_i-1) - (2q_j-1))/2 = q_i - q_j. Then r_i=s, r_j=-s; project to [-1,1] by clipping s. Also q'= (1+r_i)/2 = 0.5 + 0.5s. This projection enforces q_i+q_j=1. Sum q=n/2. This is nice. We can alternate:
1. Start q in family.
2. Perform gradient/multiplicative updates on q (or full) to reduce score.
3. Project q to anti-symmetric mean-0 family (pair average, clip).

Gradient implementation details:
```
def _conv_fft(a,b=None):
   if b is None: b=a
   na, nb = a.size, b.size
   nfft=1 << (na+nb-2).bit_length()
   fa=np.fft.rfft(a,nfft)
   fb=np.fft.rfft(b,nfft)
   c=np.fft.irfft(fa*fb,nfft)[:na+nb-1]
   return c
```
For q*q, use b=q.
For grad: d = conv_fft(q[::-1], w) full length 3n-1. y=d[n-1:2n-1]. Need clip tiny imaginary? irfft real. Roundoff may make y small negative; okay.
But `conv_fft` with a length n and b length 2n-1 gives full length 3n-1. nfft pow2 >=3n-1. For n=4096, 3n-1=12287, nfft=16384. Good.

`_lse_weights(c, tau)`:
```
cm = np.max(c)
z = (c-cm)/tau
expz = np.exp(z)
sumz = np.sum(expz)

Multiplicative LSE update full:
```
def multiplicative_update_general(p, tau, eta_list, K=None):
   p = normalize_to_sum1(p)
   c=conv_fft(p)
   M=max(c)
   # weights: soft over all c with tau
   w=softmax(c, tau) # if tau very small peaks
   d=2*corr_weights_q(w,p)
   # optional use top-K only: set w_top and scale? For target max, top-K may be better.
   dmean=np.mean(d)
   bestp=p; bestscore=score
   for eta in etas:
       p2=np.exp(np.clip(-eta*(d-dmean), -2,2))*p
       p2=normalize_to_sum1(p2)
       sc=local_score(p2)
       if sc<bestscore: best...
   return best
```

```
def normalize_to_sum1(a):
   a=np.asarray(a,float)
   if a.size==0: return None
   a=np.clip(a,0,1000)
   s=a.sum()
   if not finite or s<=1e-300: return None
   return a/s
```

Build q:
```
def build_anti_q(r,n):
   m=n//2
   q=np.empty(n,float)
   q[:m]=0.5*(1.0+r)
   q[m:]=0.5*(1.0-r[::-1])
   return q
```

Project parent to anti r:
```
def project_parent_to_r(parent, n):
   p=normalize_to_sum1(parent)
   if p is None or p.size!=n: return None
   q = p * (n/2.0)  # sum n/2
   m=n//2
   r=np.clip(q[:m] - q[m:][::-1], -1.0, 1.0)
   return r
```

def run_lbfgs(r0, tau, n, deadline):
   global RUN_DEADLINE
   RUN_DEADLINE = deadline
   bounds=[(-1.0,1.0)]* (n//2)
   options={'maxiter': 500, 'maxfun': 2000, 'ftol':1e-12, 'gtol':1e-8, 'iprint':0}
   res = minimize(anti_f, r0, jac=anti_g, args=(tau,n), method='L-BFGS-B', bounds=bounds, options=options, callback=lbfgs_callback)
   return res.x if res is not None else r0
```

```
from scipy.sparse import diags
def lp_step(p, M, seed):
   n=p.size
   if n>2048: return None
   row=2*n-1
   # For offset s, diagonal values p[s] length n
   # But diags expects list of diagonals; for s, offset=s, diagonal length row-s = n? Wait row=2n-1, main diag length n. offset s subdiag length n-s? In a row x col matrix, positive offset s means A[row=i, col=i-s] for i=s..row-1. Length row-s =2n-1-s. But we need only n-s? Because col>=0 and col<n, row<=2n-2. For offset s, col = row-s. Need 0≤col<n and 0≤row≤2n-2. row ranges s..s+n-1 (since col 0..n-1). Also row≤2n-2 automatically if s≤n-1 and col≤n-1 -> row≤s+n-1≤2n-2. So length n-s. `diags` will place n-s values at rows s..s+n-s-1? If we provide array length n-s, it starts at top? For positive offset, first element at row=s,col=0, then row=s+1,col=1, ... row=s+n-s-1=n-1,col=n-s-1. That's only up to n-1, not up to s+n-1. Wait row-col=s; if col from 0 to n-s-1, row from s to n-1. But we need rows up to s+n-1 (e.g. n=4,s=1 need rows1..4, cols0..3). Length should n? row from1 to4, col0..3 ->4 values. In general for offset s (0..n-1), length n, but last row = s+n-1 ≤2n-2, col= n-1. `diags` with length n places row=s..s+n-1. Good. For offset n-1, length n? row n-1..2n-2, col0..n-1, but col=row-s, for row=s+n-1 col=n-1, yes. So each diagonal length n.       tau_list.append(tau); tau*=0.6
   tau_list.append(0.05)
   # cap list length

      if now>=deadline: break

      sc=score_full(build_q(r_final,n))
      if sc<best_sc: best_r=r_final; best_sc=sc
   return best_r,best_sc

Let's write functions in detail.

`conv_full` with optional clip:
```
def conv_full(a,b=None):
    a=np.asarray(a,float)
    if b is None: b=a
    else: b=np.asarray(b,float)
    na=a.size; nb=b.size
    if na==0 or nb==0: return np.array([])
    nfft=1 << ((na+nb-2).bit_length())
    fa=np.fft.rfft(a,nfft)
    fb=np.fft.rfft(b,nfft)
    c=np.fft.irfft(fa*fb,nfft)[:na+nb-1]
    # clean
    c = c.real
    # If inputs nonnegative, set small negatives to 0
    if c.size and c.min() < 0:
        # tolerate only small relative
        tol = 1e-12 * max(1.0, float(np.max(c.abs()?)))
        c = np.where(c < 0, 0.0, c)
    return c
```
But if b includes weights nonnegative; ok. If inputs have tiny negative due previous? We clip inputs earlier. Use `c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)` to be safe. Could hide numerical if c should be positive but roundoff negative small; okay.

`local_score(a)`:
```
def local_score(a):
   p=normalize_to_sum1(a)
   if p is None: return inf
   c=conv_full(p)
   if c.size==0: return inf
   m=float(np.max(c))
   n=p.size
   return float(2.0*n*m)  # since sum=1
```
Since normalized sum1. Good.

`corr_from_weights(w, seq)`:
```
def corr_from_weights(w, seq):
   n=seq.size
   if n==0: return np.array([])
   d=conv_full(seq[::-1], w)  # full length 3n-1
   return d[n-1:2*n-1]
```
Check `conv_full(seq[::-1], w)` uses seq_rev nonnegative? w nonnegative, yes. But if seq has negative? Our p/q nonnegative. Good.

`_anti_fun_grad`:
```
def _anti_fun_grad(r,tau,n):
   m=n//2
   # assume r length m; clean
   r=np.asarray(r,float)
   if r.size != m: r = r[:m] or pad
   r=np.clip(r,-1.0,R_MAX)
   q=build_anti_q(r,n)
   c=conv_full(q)
   M=float(np.max(c))
   if tau <= 1e-8:
      idx=int(np.argmax(c)); w=np.zeros(c.shape); w[idx]=1.0; F=M
   else:
      z=(c-M)/tau
      expz=np.exp(z)
      s=float(np.sum(expz))
      if not finite or s<=0: w uniform; F=M
      else: w=expz/s; F=M + tau*np.log(s/len(c))
   y=corr_from_weights(w,q)
   dQ=2.0*y
   dR=0.5*(dQ[:m] - dQ[m:][::-1])
   # clean nonfinite
   dR[np.isnan(dR)] = 0; dR[np.isinf(dR)] = 0
   if not finite F: F= M
   return F, dR
```
If tau large and c-M large negative, exp underflow to0; sum at least1 (max). Good.

`anti_f` / `anti_g`:
```
def anti_f(r,tau,n):
   key=(float(tau), int(n))
   if _cache_valid(r,tau,n): return ANTI_CACHE['f']
   f,g=_anti_fun_grad(r,tau,n)
   ANTI_CACHE['valid']=True; ANTI_CACHE['key']=key; ANTI_CACHE['r']=np.asarray(r,float).copy(); ANTI_CACHE['f']=f; ANTI_CACHE['g']=g
   return f
```
But key with float tau; if tau differs at 1e-16, recompute. Use tuple. `_cache_valid` compares key.
`_cache_valid` top-level:
```
def _cache_valid(r,tau,n):
   if not ANTI_CACHE['valid']: return False
   n=int(n)
   if ANTI_CACHE['n'] != n: return False
   if abs(float(ANTI_CACHE['tau']) - float(tau)) > 1e-12: return False
   if ANTI_CACHE['r'] is None or ANTI_CACHE['r'].size != r.size: return False
   return bool(np.array_equal(ANTI_CACHE['r'], r))
```
Store separate tau,n.

`lbfgs_callback`:
```
def lbfgs_callback(_xk):
   if time.monotonic() > RUN_DEADLINE:
       return False
   return True
```

`run_lbfgs`:
```
def run_lbfgs(r0,tau,n,deadline):
   if not HAVE_SCIPY: return None
   global RUN_DEADLINE
   RUN_DEADLINE = deadline
   m=n//2
   bounds=[(-1.0,R_MAX) for _ in range(m)]
   try:
      res=minimize(anti_f, r0, jac=anti_g, args=(tau,n), method='L-BFGS-B', bounds=bounds, options={'maxiter':2000, 'maxfun':5000, 'ftol':1e-12, 'gtol':1e-8, 'iprint':0}, callback=lbfgs_callback)
      if res is not None:
         return np.asarray(res.x,float)
   except Exception:
      pass
   return None
```
Maybe set `maxiter` based on n: 2000 enough. Callback stops.

`build_anti_q` ensure r length m:
```
def build_anti_q(r,n):
   m=n//2
   r=np.asarray(r,float)
   if r.size < m: pad zeros? if >m trim.
   r=np.clip(r,-1,R_MAX)
   q=np.empty(n,float)
   q[:m]=0.5*(1+r)
   q[m:]=0.5*(1-r[::-1])
   return q
```

`score_anti_r(r,n)`:
```
q=build_anti_q(r,n); return local_score(q)
```

`project_sequence_to_r(seq,n)`:
```
def project_to_r(seq,n):
   p=normalize_to_sum1(seq)
   if p is None or p.size != n:
      # resample
      if p is not None and p.size>1:
         old=np.linspace(0,1,p.size); new=np.linspace(0,1,n)
         p=np.interp(new, old, p); p=normalize_to_sum1(p)
      else: return None
   if p is None: return None
   q = p * (n/2.0)
   m=n//2
   diff = q[:m] - q[m:][::-1]
   return np.clip(diff, -1.0, R_MAX)
```
This sums q to n/2 if p sum1. Good.

`make_r_start(n, seed, kind, parent_r)`:
Implement as top-level. Use `rng = np.random.default_rng(seed + 1000003*kind)`. m=n//2.
```
def make_r_start(n, seed, kind, parent_r=None):
   m=n//2
   rng=np.random.default_rng(seed + 1000003*(kind%11))
   kind=kind%11
   if kind==0: r=rng.uniform(-1,R_MAX,m)
   elif kind==1:
      signs=rng.choice([-1.0,1.0],m)
      amps=rng.uniform(0.6,1.0,m)
      r=signs*amps
   elif kind==2:
      f1=int(rng.integers(1,6)); f2=int(rng.integers(1,6)); ph1=2π; ph2; a1=a=rng.uniform(0.5,1); r=a1*np.sin(2π f1 arange/m+ph1)+a2*...
      r=clip
   elif kind==3:
      x=rng.normal(size=m); w=int(rng.integers(3,min(11,m+1))); kern=np.ones(w)/w; r=np.convolve(x,kern,'same'); zscore; clip
   elif kind==4:
      base=np.where(np.arange(m)%2==0,1.0,-1.0)*rng.uniform(0.4,1.0,m)
      r=base + 0.3*rng.normal(m) clip
   elif kind==5:
      if parent_r is not None: r=parent_r + 0.4*rng.normal(m); clip
      else r=rng.uniform(-1,R_MAX,m)
   elif kind==6:
      # low-discrepancy sine quadrature: r = 2*frac -1? maybe Van der Corput
      x=np.arange(m); r=np.cos(2*np.pi*x/m) * np.exp(rng.normal(0,0.3,m)) clip
   else:
      # random permutation of magnitudes sorted
      mag=rng.beta(2,2,m); ranks=...? Keep simple: mag * signs random, but order random permutation of mag? Convolution sensitive.
      mag=rng.beta(1.5,1.5,m); perm=rng.permutation(m); r=0.5*(mag[perm]*2-1)*rng.choice([-1,1],m)
   return clip
```
Ensure finite.

`generic_update`:
Need top-level `generic_multiplicative_update(p, tau_frac, K)`:
```
def generic_multiplicative_update(p, tau_frac, K=0):
   p=normalize_to_sum1(p)
   if p is None: return None,inf
   c=conv_full(p); M=float(np.max(c))
   if M<=0 or not finite: return p, local_score(p)
   n=c.size
   if K and K>0 and K<n:
      idx=np.argpartition(c, -K)[-K:]
      w=np.zeros_like(c); w[idx]=1.0/float(K)
   else:
      tau=abs(tau_frac)*max(M,1e-300)
      if tau <=0: tau=1e-300
      z=(c-M)/tau
      expz=np.exp(z); s=expz.sum()
      if s<=0 or not finite: w=np.ones(c.size)/c.size
      else: w=expz/s
   y=corr_from_weights(w,p)
   d=2.0*y
   delta=d - np.mean(d)
   scale=float(np.max(np.abs(delta)))
   if not finite or scale<=0: return p, local_score(p)
   delta/=scale
   bestp=p; bestsc=local_score(p)
   etas=(0.02,0.05,0.1,0.2,0.5,1.0,2.0,4.0)
   for eta in etas:
      p2=p*np.exp(-eta*delta)
      p2=normalize_to_sum1(p2)
      if p2 is None: continue
      sc=local_score(p2)
      if sc<bestsc:
         bestsc=sc; bestp=p2
   return bestp,bestsc
```
For normalized p, c max ~0.001. tau_frac=0.01 -> tau=1e-5; weights very peaked. Good. But if eta large and delta normalized, p2 extreme; line search picks.
Could add `K=0,tau_frac=10` uniform? Not needed.

def try_lp(p, seed):
   if not HAVE_SCIPY or not HAVE_SPARSE: return None,inf
   n=p.size
   if n>1024: return None,inf

   g=lp_direction(p,M,seed)
   if g is None: return None,inf
   gn=normalize_to_sum1(g)

   return best,bestsc

def anti_active_update(r, K):
   q=build(n); c; M; if M<=0 return r, score
   K=max(1,int(K))

def generate_r_starts(n, count, seed, parent_r, extra_starts):
    starts=[]
    if parent_r is not None:
       starts.append(np.clip(parent_r,-1,R_MAX))
    if extra_starts:
       for seq in extra_starts:
          r=project_to_r(seq,n)
          if r is not None: starts.append(r)
    # if too many extras, limit first 5
    starts = starts[:5]
    k=0
    while len(starts)<count:
       starts.append(make_r_start(n, seed + 12997*len(starts), k, parent_r))
       k+=1
    return starts[:count]

def _anti_perturb(r, rng, n):
   m=r.size
   w=int(rng.integers(max(1,m//20), max(2,m//2)))
   idx=rng.choice(m, size=w, replace=False)
   rn=r.copy()
   # Each perturbed value: sometimes flip sign around 0? For anti sequence, values in [-1,RMAX]
   delta = rng.normal(0, scale, w)
   rn[idx] = np.clip(rn[idx]+delta, -1, R_MAX)
   # occasional binary flip: set to sign*1
   if rng.random()<0.3:
       flips=rng.integers(0,w//2+1)
       idx2=idx[:flips]
       rn[idx2]=np.where(np.sign(rn[idx2] - 0.5)>0, -1.0, 1.0)? Hard.
   return rn
```

`_legendre_r` top-level:

def legendre_r_start(m, seed):
   rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
   p=next_prime(m+1)
   L=p-1
   shift=int(rng.integers(0,L))
   comp=1.0 if rng.random()<0.5 else -1.0
   amp=float(rng.uniform(0.85,1.0))
   out=np.empty(m)
   for i in range(m):
      a=(shift+i) % L
      if a==0: val=1.0
      else: val=1.0 if pow(a,p-2,p)==1 else -1.0
      out[i]=comp*val*amp
   return np.clip(out,-1,R_MAX)
```
For p=257 pow each 256, trivial. For p=1031, 1024 pows okay.

def balanced_greedy_r(m, seed):
  rng; prefix=0; out=[]
  for i in range(m):
     # choose sign to reduce prefix; random perturbation
     if prefix >=0:
        r0=-1; r1=1
     else: r0=1; r1=-1
     if rng.random()<0.25: val=r1
     else: val=r0
     # add continuous noise
     val += rng.normal(0,0.2)
     prefix += val
     out.append(val)
  return clip
```

Detailed function code:

```
def _pow2_len(n):
    n=int(n)
    if n <=0: return 1
    return 1 << (n-1).bit_length()
```

`conv_full`:
```
def conv_full(a,b=None):
    a=np.asarray(a, dtype=float).ravel()
    if b is None:
        b=a
    else:
        b=np.asarray(b, dtype=float).ravel()
    if a.size==0 or b.size==0:
        return np.array([], dtype=float)
    nfft=_pow2_len(a.size+b.size-1)
    fa=np.fft.rfft(a, n=nfft)
    fb=np.fft.rfft(b, n=nfft)
    c=np.fft.irfft(fa*fb, n=nfft)[:a.size+b.size-1]
    c=np.asarray(c, dtype=float)
    if c.size:
       c = np.where(c < 0.0, 0.0, c)
    return c
```
Setting all negatives to0; if due actual negative? inputs nonnegative; w nonnegative. ok.

`normalize_to_sum1`:
```
def normalize_to_sum1(a):
   a=np.asarray(a,dtype=float).ravel()
   if a.size==0: return None
   if not np.all(np.isfinite(a)): return None
   a=np.clip(a,0.0,1000.0)
   s=float(np.sum(a))
   if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300: return None
   return a/s
```

`local_score`:
```
def local_score(a):
   p=normalize_to_sum1(a)
   if p is None: return float('inf')
   c=conv_full(p)
   if c.size==0: return float('inf')
   m=float(np.max(c))
   return float(2.0*p.size*m)
```

`corr_from_weights`:
```
def corr_from_weights(w, seq):
   seq=np.asarray(seq,dtype=float).ravel(); w=...ravel
   n=seq.size
   if n==0: return np.array([],float)
   d=conv_full(seq[::-1], w)
   if d.size < 2*n-1:
      # fallback direct? pad ones
      y=np.convolve(seq[::-1], w)[:?]
   return d[n-1:2*n-1]
```
Should be enough.

`_is_prime`:
```
def _is_prime(x):
   if x<2: return False
   if x%2==0: return x==2
   d=3
   while d*d<=x:
      if x%d==0: return False
      d+=2
   return True
def _next_prime(x):
   x=max(2,int(x))
   if x%2==0: x-=1
   while not _is_prime(x): x+=2
   return x
```
For x=2 returns? x%2=0 -> x=1, while not prime -> x=3; but next_prime(2) returns3. We need prime at least n. If n=2, maybe return2. Implement:
```
if x<=2: return 2
if x%2==0: x+=1
while not _is_prime(x): x+=2
return x
```

`project_to_r` top-level:
```
def project_to_r(seq,n):
   p=normalize_to_sum1(seq)
   if p is None: return None
   if p.size != n:
      if p.size == 1:
         p = np.full(n, float(p.flat[0]))/n
      else:
         old=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,p.size)
         new=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,n)
         p=np.interp(new, old, p)
         p=normalize_to_sum1(p)
   if p is None or p.size != n: return None
   q = p * (0.5*n)
   m=n//2
   diff = q[:m] - q[m:][::-1]
   return np.clip(diff, -1.0, R_MAX)
```
If n odd? We only even. If n odd fallback? Not use. if n<2 return None.

`build_anti_q` for n even:
```
def build_anti_q(r,n):
   m=n//2
   r=np.asarray(r,dtype=float).ravel()
   if r.size < m: pad
   else: r=r[:m]
   r=np.clip(r,-1,R_MAX)
   q=np.empty(n,float)
   q[:m]=0.5*(1+r)
   q[m:]=0.5*(1-r[::-1])
   return q
```
If n odd? Maybe not. Could handle by using m=(n+1)//2 with middle 0? But we only call even. For safety, if n%2: m=n//2, middle q[m]=0.5? To avoid errors. Add:
```
if n%2:
   q=np.empty(n); q[:m]=...; q[m:]=... # lengths? m + m +1? 
   q[m] = 0.5
   q[m+1:] = 0.5*(1 - r[::-1])? But not needed.
```
We can just ensure even.

Anti functional:
```
ANTI_CACHE = {'valid':False,'tau':0.0,'n':0,'r':None,'f':float('inf'),'g':None}

def _anti_cache_hit(r,tau,n):
   if not ANTI_CACHE['valid']: return False
   if int(ANTI_CACHE['n']) != int(n): return False
   if abs(float(ANTI_CACHE['tau']) - float(tau)) > 1e-9: return False
   cr=ANTI_CACHE['r']
   if cr is None: return False
   r=np.asarray(r,float).ravel()
   if cr.size != r.size: return False
   return bool(np.array_equal(cr, r))
```
`_anti_fun_grad` as above. Need handle tau as float.
In `_anti_fun_grad`, if r size mismatch, call build? Build clips/pads; computing gradient uses adjusted r. But cache key with original r; if mismatch, level's r length always right. ok.

`anti_f` and `anti_g`:
```
def anti_f(r, tau, n):
   if _anti_cache_hit(r,tau,n): return ANTI_CACHE['f']
   f,g = _anti_fun_grad(r,tau,n)
   ANTI_CACHE['valid']=True
   ANTI_CACHE['tau']=float(tau)
   ANTI_CACHE['n']=int(n)
   ANTI_CACHE['r']=np.asarray(r,float).ravel().copy()
   ANTI_CACHE['f']=f
   ANTI_CACHE['g']=g
   return f
```
In `anti_g`, return g.

`run_lbfgs`: Need `minimize` imported. If not, return None.
```
def run_lbfgs(r0, tau, n, deadline):
   if not HAVE_SCIPY: return None
   global RUN_DEADLINE
   RUN_DEADLINE = float(deadline)
   m=n//2
   bounds=[(-1.0, R_MAX) for _ in range(m)]
   options={'maxiter': 1000, 'maxfun': 3000, 'ftol':1e-12, 'gtol':1e-8, 'iprint':0, 'maxls':50}
   try:
      res = minimize(anti_f, r0, jac=anti_g, args=(float(tau), int(n)), method='L-BFGS-B', bounds=bounds, options=options, callback=lbfgs_callback)
   except Exception:
      return None
   if res is not None and hasattr(res,'x'):
      x=np.asarray(res.x,float).ravel()
      if x.size==m and np.all(np.isfinite(x)): return x
   return None
```
`callback` returns False after deadline. Also maybe print every 100 loops? We can have global `LBFGS_CALLS` but not needed.

`anti_active_update`:
```
def anti_active_update(r, K, n):
   r=np.asarray(r,float).ravel()
   q=build_anti_q(r,n)
   c=conv_full(q)
   M=float(np.max(c))
   if not finite or M<=0: return r, local_score(q)
   K=int(max(1,K))
   if K>=c.size:
      idx=np.nonzero(c>0.5*M)[0]
      if idx.size==0: idx=np.array([int(np.argmax(c))])
   else:
      idx=np.argpartition(c, -K)[-K:]
   w=np.zeros_like(c); w[idx]=1.0/float(idx.size)
   y=corr_from_weights(w,q)
   m=n//2
   dQ=2*y
   dR=0.5*(dQ[:m]-dQ[m:][::-1])
   if not finite: return r, score
   scale=float(np.max(np.abs(dR)))
   if scale<=0: return r, score
   dR=dR/scale
   best_r=r; best_sc=local_score(q)
   etas=(0.005,0.01,0.02,0.05,0.1,0.2,0.5,1.0,2.0)
   for eta in etas:
      rn=np.clip(r - eta*dR, -1.0, R_MAX)
      sc=local_score(build_anti_q(rn,n))
      if sc<best_sc: best_sc=sc; best_r=rn.copy()
   return best_r,best_sc
```
Could be costly (9 scores per update) but active used limited.

def optimize_r_anneal(r0, n, time_budget, seed):
   t0=time.monotonic(); deadline=t0+float(time_budget)
   r0=np.asarray(r0,float).ravel(); m=n//2
   if r0.size<m: pad else trim
   r0=np.clip(r0,-1,R_MAX)
   best_r=r0.copy(); best_sc=score_anti_r(best_r,n)
   rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
   def cannot define nested.
   # active polish before
   for _ in range(5):
      if time.monotonic() >= deadline - 1: break
      K=int(rng.choice(np.array([8,32,128], dtype=int)))
      r2,sc2=anti_active_update(best_r,K,n)
      if sc2<best_sc: best_r,best_sc=r2,sc2
   if time_budget <= 5 or not HAVE_SCIPY:
      # continue active until deadline
      while time.monotonic() < deadline:
         K=...
         r2,sc2=anti_active_update(...)
         update or break if no improve 20
      return best_r,best_sc
   # compute tau
   q=build_anti_q(best_r,n); c=conv_full(q); maxc=float(np.max(c))
   tau=max(1.0, 0.08*maxc)
   taus=[]
   while tau > 0.08 and len(taus) < 4:
       taus.append(float(tau)); tau *= 0.55
   taus.append(float(max(0.03, tau)))
   # maybe append 0.05
   # loop
   last_no=0
   for tau in taus:
      rem=deadline - time.monotonic()
      if rem < 4: break
      run_time = min(rem, max(2.0, rem / float(len(taus))))
      r_run = run_lbfgs(best_r, tau, n, time.monotonic() + run_time)
      if r_run is not None:
          sc=score_anti_r(r_run,n)
          if sc<best_sc: best_r,best_sc=r_run,sc
      # active polish small
      for _ in range(3):
          rem=deadline - time.monotonic()
          if rem<2: break
          K=int(rng.choice(...))
          r2,sc2=anti_active_update(best_r,K,n)
          update
   # final active until deadline but cap
   no=0
   while time.monotonic() < deadline:
      K=int(rng.choice(...))
      r2,sc2=anti_active_update(best_r,K,n)
      if sc2<best_sc: best_r,best_sc=r2,sc2; no=0
      else: no+=1
      if no>30: break
   return best_r,best_sc
```

```
def try_lp(p, seed):
   if not (HAVE_SCIPY and HAVE_SPARSE): return None,inf
   n=p.size
   if n>1024: return None,inf
   c=conv_full(p); M=float(np.max(c))
   g=lp_direction(p,M,seed)
   if g is None: return None,inf
   gn=normalize_to_sum1(g)
   if gn is None: return None,inf
   best=p; best_sc=local_score(p)
   for t in (0.1,0.2,0.35,0.5,0.7,1.0):
       cand=normalize_to_sum1((1.0-t)*p + t*gn)
       if cand is None: continue
       sc=local_score(cand)
       if sc<best_sc: best,cand; update
   return best,best_sc
```
`lp_direction` imports from scipy.sparse `diags`. If no `diags`, fail.
```
def lp_direction(p,M,seed):
   try:
      n=p.size; mrows=2*n-1
      vals=[]; offs=[]
      for s in range(n): vals.append(float(p[s])); offs.append(s)
      A=diags(vals, offs, shape=(mrows,n), format='csr')
   except Exception: return None
   rng=...
   cvec=-np.ones(n) + 1e-9*rng.uniform(...)
   bvec=np.full(mrows, M*(1+1e-7))
   bounds=[(0.0,1.0)]*n
   try: res=linprog(cvec, A_ub=A, b_ub=bvec, bounds=bounds, method='highs', options={'presolve':True,'disp':False})
   except: return None
   if res is not None and res.success:
      x=np.asarray(res.x,float).ravel()
      x=np.clip(x,0,1)
      if np.all(np.isfinite(x)) and np.sum(x)>0: return x
   return None
```
Potential issue `diags` with list of scalars `vals` and offsets `offs`: If vals is list of floats, works. But for each offset, scalar fills diagonal length? In scipy, if a scalar is given, it is used for the whole diagonal. Good.

`generic_search`:
```
def generic_search(p, budget, seed):
   p=normalize_to_sum1(p)
   if p is None: return None,inf
   best=p; best_sc=local_score(p)
   t0=now; deadline=t0+budget
   rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
   tau_opts=(0.005,0.02,0.06,0.2,0.6)
   k_opts=(4,16,64,256)
   it=0; last_lp=0.0; no_improve=0
   while now < deadline:
      it +=1
      rem=deadline-now
      if rem < 0.5: break
      if it % 3 == 0:
          # LP
          if n<=1024 and (now - last_lp) > min(20.0, max(5.0, budget/10)):
             cand,sc=try_lp(best, seed+it)
             if sc<best_sc: update; no_improve=0
             last_lp=now
             continue
      if it % 2 == 0:
          K=int(rng.choice(k_opts)); tau=0.0
      else:
          K=0; tau=float(rng.choice(tau_opts))
      cand,sc=generic_multiplicative_update(best, tau, K)
      if sc<best_sc: update; no_improve=0
      else: no_improve+=1
      if no_improve > 25:
          noise=np.exp(rng.normal(0.0,0.3,best.size))
          cand=normalize_to_sum1(best*noise)
          if cand is not None:
             sc=local_score(cand)
             if sc<best_sc: update
          no_improve=0
   return best,best_sc
```
This uses one update per loop; for budget150s, maybe hundreds updates. Good.

```python
"""
Search for low-score non-negative step-function coefficients.

Objective:
    score = 2*n*max(convolve(a,a)) / sum(a)^2.

This implementation focuses on a structurally useful family:
    n even, a_i + a_{n-1-i} = 1/2 (after scaling so sum(a)=n/2),
which is equivalent to r_i = -r_{n-1-i}, -1 <= r_i <= 2, and
    q_i = (1 + r_i)/2  for i < n/2,
    q_{n-1-i} = (1 - r_i)/2.

Within that family the score is driven by small "excess" convolution mass.
The program optimizes the r variables with L-BFGS-B on a log-sum-exp surrogate,
polishes with active-peak projected-gradient steps, then relaxes the anti-pair
constraint with a full-sequence local optimizer and sparse LP direction steps.

Various lengths are searched, starting from the parent sequence when available.
"""

import time
import numpy as np

try:
    from scipy.optimize import minimize, linprog
    HAVE_SCIPY = True
except Exception:
    HAVE_SCIPY = False

try:
    from scipy.sparse import diags
    HAVE_SPARSE = True
except Exception:
    HAVE_SPARSE = False


R_MAX = 2.0
RUN_DEADLINE = 0.0
ANTI_CACHE = {
    "valid": False,
    "tau": 0.0,
    "n": 0,
    "r": None,
    "f": float("inf"),
    "g": None,
}


def _pow2_len(n):
    n = int(n)
    if n <= 0:
        return 1
    return 1 << (n - 1).bit_length()


def conv_full(a, b=None):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=float).ravel()
    if b is None:
        b = a
    else:
        b = np.asarray(b, dtype=float).ravel()
    if a.size == 0 or b.size == 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=float)
    need = a.size + b.size - 1
    nfft = _pow2_len(need)
    fa = np.fft.rfft(a, n=nfft)
    fb = np.fft.rfft(b, n=nfft)
    c = np.fft.irfft(fa * fb, n=nfft)[:need]
    c = np.asarray(c, dtype=float)
    if c.size:
        c = np.where(c < 0.0, 0.0, c)
    return c


def normalize_to_sum1(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=float).ravel()
    if a.size == 0:
        return None
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(a)):
        return None
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = float(np.sum(a))
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
        return None
    return a / s


def local_score(a):
    p = normalize_to_sum1(a)
    if p is None:
        return float("inf")
    c = conv_full(p)
    if c.size == 0:
        return float("inf")
    m = float(np.max(c))
    if not np.isfinite(m):
        return float("inf")
    return float(2.0 * p.size * m)


def corr_from_weights(w, seq):
    seq = np.asarray(seq, dtype=float).ravel()
    w = np.asarray(w, dtype=float).ravel()
    n = seq.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=float)
    d = conv_full(seq[::-1], w)
    if d.size < 2 * n - 1:
        # Fallback for pathological edge cases.
        d = np.convolve(seq[::-1], w, mode="full")
    return d[n - 1:2 * n - 1]


def _is_prime(x):
    x = int(x)
    if x < 2:
        return False
    if x % 2 == 0:
        return x == 2
    d = 3
    while d * d <= x:
        if x % d == 0:
            return False
        d += 2
    return True


def _next_prime(x):
    x = int(x)
    if x <= 2:
        return 2
    if x % 2 == 0:
        x += 1
    while not _is_prime(x):
        x += 2
    return x


def legendre_r_start(m, seed):
    m = max(1, int(m))
    rng = np.random.default_rng(int(seed) + 811)
    p = _next_prime(m + 1)
    L = max(1, p - 1)
    shift = int(rng.integers(0, L))
    comp = 1.0 if rng.random() < 0.5 else -1.0
    amp = float(rng.uniform(0.85, 1.0))
    out = np.empty(m, dtype=float)
    for i in range(m):
        a = (shift + i) % L
        if a == 0:
            val = 1.0
        else:
            val = 1.0 if pow(a, p - 2, p) == 1 else -1.0
        out[i] = comp * amp * val
    return np.clip(out, -1.0, R_MAX)


def make_r_start(n, seed, kind, parent_r=None):
    m = max(1, int(n) // 2)
    kind = int(kind) % 12
    rng = np.random.default_rng(int(seed) + 1000003 * kind)
    idx = np.arange(m, dtype=float)

    if kind == 0:
        r = rng.uniform(-1.0, R_MAX, m)

    elif kind == 1:
        signs = rng.choice(np.array([-1.0, 1.0]), size=m)
        amps = rng.uniform(0.55, 1.0, m)
        r = signs * amps

    elif kind == 2:
        f1 = float(rng.integers(1, 7))
        f2 = float(rng.integers(1, 7))
        p1 = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi))
        p2 = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi))
        a1 = float(rng.uniform(0.4, 0.9))
        a2 = float(rng.uniform(0.1, 0.6))
        r = a1 * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * f1 * idx / max(1, m) + p1) + a2 * np.sin(
            2.0 * np.pi * f2 * idx / max(1, m) + p2
        )

    elif kind == 3:
        x = rng.normal(size=m)
        w = int(rng.integers(3, max(4, min(13, m + 1))))
        kern = np.ones(w, dtype=float) / float(w)
        r = np.convolve(x, kern, mode="same")
        mean = float(np.mean(r))
        std = float(np.std(r))
        if std > 1e-12:
            r = 1.0 + 0.8 * (r - mean) / std
        else:
            r = rng.uniform(-1.0, R_MAX, m)

    elif kind == 4:
        base = np.where((np.arange(m) % 2) == 0, 1.0, -1.0)
        amp = rng.uniform(0.35, 1.0, m)
        r = base * amp + 0.35 * rng.normal(size=m)

    elif kind == 5:
        if parent_r is not None:
            r = np.asarray(parent_r, dtype=float).ravel()
            if r.size != m:
                if r.size == 1:
                    r = np.full(m, float(r.flat[0]), dtype=float)
                else:
                    old = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, r.size)
                    new = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, m)
                    r = np.interp(new, old, r)
            r = r + 0.35 * rng.normal(size=m)
        else:
            r = rng.uniform(-1.0, R_MAX, m)

    elif kind == 6:
        f = float(rng.integers(1, 9))
        ph = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi))
        r = 0.9 * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * f * idx / max(1, m) + ph)
        r += 0.25 * rng.normal(size=m)

    elif kind == 7:
        mag = rng.beta(1.7, 1.7, m)
        mag = 2.0 * mag - 1.0
        perm = rng.permutation(m)
        signs = rng.choice(np.array([-1.0, 1.0]), size=m)
        r = mag[perm] * signs

    elif kind == 8:
        r_tmp = np.empty(m, dtype=float)
        pref = 0.0
        for i in range(m):
            if pref >= 0.0:
                primary, secondary = -1.0, 1.0
            else:
                primary, secondary = 1.0, -1.0
            if rng.random() < 0.22:
                v = secondary
            else:
                v = primary
            v += 0.30 * rng.normal()
            pref += v
            r_tmp[i] = v
        r = r_tmp

    elif kind == 9:
        r = legendre_r_start(m, seed)

    elif kind == 10:
        r = legendre_r_start(m, seed) + 0.22 * rng.normal(size=m)

    else:
        r = rng.uniform(-1.0, R_MAX, m)

    r = np.asarray(r, dtype=float).ravel()
    if r.size != m:
        if r.size == 1:
            r = np.full(m, float(r.flat[0]), dtype=float)
        else:
            old = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, r.size)
            new = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, m)
            r = np.interp(new, old, r)
    return np.clip(r, -1.0, R_MAX)


def project_to_r(seq, n):
    if n is None or int(n) % 2 != 0 or int(n) < 4:
        return None
    n = int(n)
    p = normalize_to_sum1(seq)
    if p is None:
        return None
    if p.size != n:
        if p.size == 1:
            p = np.full(n, float(p.flat[0]), dtype=float) / float(n)
        else:
            old = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, p.size)
            new = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
            p = np.interp(new, old, p)
        p = normalize_to_sum1(p)
        if p is None:
            return None
    q = p * (0.5 * float(n))
    m = n // 2
    diff = q[:m] - q[m:][::-1]
    return np.clip(diff, -1.0, R_MAX)


def build_anti_q(r, n):
    n = int(n)
    if n % 2 != 0 or n < 2:
        # Not used in the main path, but keep safe.
        m = max(1, n // 2)
        r = np.asarray(r, dtype=float).ravel()
        if r.size < m:
            pad = np.zeros(m - r.size, dtype=float)
            r = np.concatenate((r, pad))
        else:
            r = r[:m]
        r = np.clip(r, -1.0, R_MAX)
        q = np.full(n, 0.5, dtype=float)
        q[:m] = 0.5 * (1.0 + r)
        if n > 2 * m:
            q[m + 1:] = 0.5 * (1.0 - r[::-1])
        return q

    m = n // 2
    r = np.asarray(r, dtype=float).ravel()
    if r.size < m:
        pad = np.zeros(m - r.size, dtype=float)
        r = np.concatenate((r, pad))
    else:
        r = r[:m]
    r = np.clip(r, -1.0, R_MAX)
    q = np.empty(n, dtype=float)
    q[:m] = 0.5 * (1.0 + r)
    q[m:] = 0.5 * (1.0 - r[::-1])
    return q


def score_anti_r(r, n):
    q = build_anti_q(r, n)
    return local_score(q)


def _anti_cache_hit(r, tau, n):
    if not ANTI_CACHE["valid"]:
        return False
    if int(ANTI_CACHE["n"]) != int(n):
        return False
    if abs(float(ANTI_CACHE["tau"]) - float(tau)) > 1e-9:
        return False
    cr = ANTI_CACHE["r"]
    if cr is None:
        return False
    r = np.asarray(r, dtype=float).ravel()
    if cr.size != r.size:
        return False
    return bool(np.array_equal(cr, r))


def _anti_fun_grad(r, tau, n):
    n = int(n)
    m = n // 2
    r = np.asarray(r, dtype=float).ravel()
    if r.size < m:
        pad = np.zeros(m - r.size, dtype=float)
        r = np.concatenate((r, pad))
    else:
        r = r[:m]
    r = np.clip(r, -1.0, R_MAX)

    q = build_anti_q(r, n)
    c = conv_full(q)
    M = float(np.max(c)) if c.size else 0.0
    if not np.isfinite(M) or M <= 0.0:
        F = M if np.isfinite(M) else 0.0
        return F, np.zeros(m, dtype=float)

    tau = float(tau)
    if tau <= 1e-8:
        idx = int(np.argmax(c))
        w = np.zeros_like(c, dtype=float)
        w[idx] = 1.0
        F = M
    else:
        z = (c - M) / tau
        expz = np.exp(z)
        s = float(np.sum(expz))
        if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
            w = np.ones_like(c, dtype=float)
            w /= float(c.size)
            F = M
        else:
            w = expz / s
            F = M + tau * float(np.log(s / float(c.size)))

    y = corr_from_weights(w, q)
    dQ = 2.0 * y
    dR = 0.5 * (dQ[:m] - dQ[m:][::-1])
    dR = np.where(np.isfinite(dR), dR, 0.0)
    if not np.isfinite(F):
        F = M
        dR = np.where(np.isfinite(dR), dR, 0.0)
    return float(F), dR


def anti_f(r, tau, n):
    if _anti_cache_hit(r, tau, n):
        return ANTI_CACHE["f"]
    f, g = _anti_fun_grad(r, tau, n)
    ANTI_CACHE["valid"] = True
    ANTI_CACHE["tau"] = float(tau)
    ANTI_CACHE["n"] = int(n)
    ANTI_CACHE["r"] = np.asarray(r, dtype=float).ravel().copy()
    ANTI_CACHE["f"] = float(f)
    ANTI_CACHE["g"] = g
    return float(f)


def anti_g(r, tau, n):
    if _anti_cache_hit(r, tau, n):
        return ANTI_CACHE["g"]
    f, g = _anti_fun_grad(r, tau, n)
    ANTI_CACHE["valid"] = True
    ANTI_CACHE["tau"] = float(tau)
    ANTI_CACHE["n"] = int(n)
    ANTI_CACHE["r"] = np.asarray(r, dtype=float).ravel().copy()
    ANTI_CACHE["f"] = float(f)
    ANTI_CACHE["g"] = g
    return g


def lbfgs_callback(_xk):
    if time.monotonic() > RUN_DEADLINE:
        return False
    return True


def run_lbfgs(r0, tau, n, deadline):
    if not HAVE_SCIPY:
        return None
    global RUN_DEADLINE
    RUN_DEADLINE = float(deadline)
    m = int(n) // 2
    bounds = [(-1.0, R_MAX) for _ in range(m)]
    options = {
        "maxiter": 1000,
        "maxfun": 3000,
        "ftol": 1e-12,
        "gtol": 1e-8,
        "iprint": 0,
        "maxls": 50,
    }
    try:
        res = minimize(
            anti_f,
            r0,
            jac=anti_g,
            args=(float(tau), int(n)),
            method="L-BFGS-B",
            bounds=bounds,
            options=options,
            callback=lbfgs_callback,
        )
    except Exception:
        return None
    if res is not None and hasattr(res, "x"):
        x = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=float).ravel()
        if x.size == m and np.all(np.isfinite(x)):
            return x
    return None


def anti_active_update(r, K, n):
    r = np.asarray(r, dtype=float).ravel()
    q = build_anti_q(r, n)
    c = conv_full(q)
    M = float(np.max(c)) if c.size else 0.0
    base_sc = local_score(q)
    if not np.isfinite(M) or M <= 0.0:
        return r, base_sc

    K = int(max(1, K))
    if K >= c.size:
        idx = np.nonzero(c > 0.5 * M)[0]
        if idx.size == 0:
            idx = np.array([int(np.argmax(c))], dtype=int)
    else:
        idx = np.argpartition(c, -K)[-K:]
    w = np.zeros_like(c, dtype=float)
    w[idx] = 1.0 / float(idx.size)

    y = corr_from_weights(w, q)
    m = n // 2
    dQ = 2.0 * y
    dR = 0.5 * (dQ[:m] - dQ[m:][::-1])
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(dR)):
        return r, base_sc
    scale = float(np.max(np.abs(dR)))
    if not np.isfinite(scale) or scale <= 0.0:
        return r, base_sc
    dR = dR / scale

    best_r = r.copy()
    best_sc = base_sc
    etas = (0.005, 0.01, 0.02, 0.05, 0.1, 0.2, 0.5, 1.0, 2.0)
    for eta in etas:
        rn = np.clip(r - eta * dR, -1.0, R_MAX)
        sc = score_anti_r(rn, n)
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc:
            best_sc = float(sc)
            best_r = rn.copy()
    return best_r, best_sc


def optimize_r_anneal(r0, n, time_budget, seed):
    n = int(n)
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    deadline = t0 + float(max(0.0, time_budget))
    m = n // 2

    r = np.asarray(r0, dtype=float).ravel()
    if r.size < m:
        pad = np.zeros(m - r.size, dtype=float)
        r = np.concatenate((r, pad))
    else:
        r = r[:m]
    r = np.clip(r, -1.0, R_MAX)

    best_r = r.copy()
    best_sc = score_anti_r(best_r, n)
    rng = np.random.default_rng(int(seed) + 333)
    kc = np.array([8, 32, 128, 256], dtype=int)

    for _ in range(4):
        if time.monotonic() >= deadline - 1.0:
            break
        K = int(rng.choice(kc))
        r2, sc2 = anti_active_update(best_r, K, n)
        if np.isfinite(sc2) and sc2 < best_sc:
            best_r = r2
            best_sc = float(sc2)

    if time_budget <= 5.0 or not HAVE_SCIPY:
        no = 0
        while time.monotonic() < deadline:
            K = int(rng.choice(kc))
            r2, sc2 = anti_active_update(best_r, K, n)
            if np.isfinite(sc2) and sc2 < best_sc:
                best_r = r2
                best_sc = float(sc2)
                no = 0
            else:
                no += 1
            if no > 35:
                break
        return best_r, best_sc

    q = build_anti_q(best_r, n)
    c = conv_full(q)
    maxc = float(np.max(c)) if c.size else 1.0
    tau = max(1.0, 0.08 * maxc)
    taus = []
    while tau > 0.08 and len(taus) < 4:
        taus.append(float(tau))
        tau *= 0.55
    taus.append(float(max(0.03, tau)))

    for tau in taus:
        rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
        if rem < 4.0:
            break
        run_time = min(rem, max(2.0, rem / float(len(taus))))
        r_run = run_lbfgs(best_r, tau, n, time.monotonic() + run_time)
        if r_run is not None:
            sc_run = score_anti_r(r_run, n)
            if np.isfinite(sc_run) and sc_run < best_sc:
                best_r = r_run
                best_sc = float(sc_run)

        for _ in range(3):
            rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
            if rem < 2.0:
                break
            K = int(rng.choice(kc))
            r2, sc2 = anti_active_update(best_r, K, n)
            if np.isfinite(sc2) and sc2 < best_sc:
                best_r = r2
                best_sc = float(sc2)

    no = 0
    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        K = int(rng.choice(kc))
        r2, sc2 = anti_active_update(best_r, K, n)
        if np.isfinite(sc2) and sc2 < best_sc:
            best_r = r2
            best_sc = float(sc2)
            no = 0
        else:
            no += 1
        if no > 40:
            break

    return best_r, best_sc


def optimize_anti_length(n, budget, seed, parent_arr=None, extra_starts=None):
    n = int(n)
    if n < 4:
        return None, float("inf")
    if n % 2 != 0:
        n -= 1
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    deadline = t0 + float(max(0.0, budget))
    if deadline - t0 < 5.0:
        return None, float("inf")

    parent_r = None
    if parent_arr is not None:
        parent_r = project_to_r(parent_arr, n)

    if budget < 50.0:
        count = 0
    elif n <= 600:
        count = min(25, max(3, int(budget / 28.0)))
    elif n <= 1200:
        count = min(16, max(2, int(budget / 45.0)))
    elif n <= 2500:
        count = min(10, max(2, int(budget / 70.0)))
    else:
        count = min(6, max(1, int(budget / 90.0)))

    starts = []
    if parent_r is not None:
        starts.append(np.asarray(parent_r, dtype=float).ravel())
    if extra_starts:
        for s in extra_starts[:4]:
            r = project_to_r(s, n)
            if r is not None:
                starts.append(r)
    starts.append(legendre_r_start(n // 2, int(seed) + 17))
    k = 0
    while len(starts) < count and count > 0:
        starts.append(make_r_start(n, int(seed) + 13193 * len(starts) + 1, k, parent_r))
        k += 1

    best_r = None
    best_sc = float("inf")

    for idx, r0 in enumerate(starts):
        rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
        if rem < 8.0:
            break
        chunk = rem / float(len(starts) - idx)
        if n <= 600:
            cap = 45.0
        elif n <= 1200:
            cap = 80.0
        elif n <= 2500:
            cap = 115.0
        else:
            cap = 150.0
        chunk = min(chunk, rem, cap)
        if chunk < 5.0:
            chunk = min(rem, 5.0)
        try:
            r_best, sc_best = optimize_r_anneal(r0, n, chunk, int(seed) + 100 * idx + 7)
        except Exception:
            r_best, sc_best = None, float("inf")
        if r_best is not None and np.isfinite(sc_best) and sc_best < best_sc:
            best_r = r_best
            best_sc = float(sc_best)

    if best_r is None:
        try:
            r0 = legendre_r_start(n // 2, int(seed) + 555)
            r_best, sc_best = optimize_r_anneal(r0, n, min(30.0, deadline - time.monotonic()), seed)
        except Exception:
            r_best, sc_best = None, float("inf")
        if r_best is not None and np.isfinite(sc_best):
            best_r = r_best
            best_sc = float(sc_best)

    if best_r is None:
        return None, float("inf")

    rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
    if rem > 5.0:
        rng = np.random.default_rng(int(seed) + 919)
        kc = np.array([8, 32, 128, 256], dtype=int)
        no = 0
        while rem > 2.0:
            K = int(rng.choice(kc))
            r2, sc2 = anti_active_update(best_r, K, n)
            if np.isfinite(sc2) and sc2 < best_sc:
                best_r = r2
                best_sc = float(sc2)
                no = 0
            else:
                no += 1
            if no > 50:
                break
            rem = deadline - time.monotonic()

    p = normalize_to_sum1(build_anti_q(best_r, n))
    if p is None:
        return None, float("inf")
    return p, local_score(p)


def lp_direction(p, M, seed):
    if not (HAVE_SCIPY and HAVE_SPARSE):
        return None
    p = normalize_to_sum1(p)
    if p is None:
        return None
    n = p.size
    if n < 4 or n > 1024:
        return None
    try:
        mrows = 2 * n - 1
        vals = [float(x) for x in p]
        offs = list(range(n))
        A = diags(vals, offs, shape=(mrows, n), format="csr")
    except Exception:
        return None

    rng = np.random.default_rng(int(seed) + 271828)
    cvec = -np.ones(n, dtype=float)
    cvec += 1e-9 * rng.uniform(-1.0, 1.0, n)
    bvec = np.full(mrows, float(M) * (1.0 + 1e-7), dtype=float)
    bounds = [(0.0, 1.0) for _ in range(n)]
    try:
        res = linprog(
            cvec,
            A_ub=A,
            b_ub=bvec,
            bounds=bounds,
            method="highs",
            options={"presolve": True, "disp": False},
        )
    except Exception:
        return None
    if res is not None and res.success:
        x = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=float).ravel()
        x = np.clip(x, 0.0, 1.0)
        if x.size == n and np.all(np.isfinite(x)) and float(np.sum(x)) > 0.0:
            return x
    return None


def try_lp(p, seed):
    if not (HAVE_SCIPY and HAVE_SPARSE):
        return None, float("inf")
    p = normalize_to_sum1(p)
    if p is None or p.size > 1024:
        return None, float("inf")
    c = conv_full(p)
    M = float(np.max(c)) if c.size else 0.0
    if not np.isfinite(M) or M <= 0.0:
        return None, float("inf")
    g = lp_direction(p, M, seed)
    if g is None:
        return None, float("inf")
    gn = normalize_to_sum1(g)
    if gn is None:
        return None, float("inf")
    best = p
    best_sc = local_score(p)
    for t in (0.1, 0.2, 0.35, 0.5, 0.7, 1.0):
        cand = normalize_to_sum1((1.0 - t) * p + t * gn)
        if cand is None:
            continue
        sc = local_score(cand)
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc:
            best = cand
            best_sc = float(sc)
    return best, best_sc


def generic_multiplicative_update(p, tau_frac, K):
    p = normalize_to_sum1(p)
    if p is None:
        return None, float("inf")
    c = conv_full(p)
    if c.size == 0:
        return p, float("inf")
    M = float(np.max(c))
    if not np.isfinite(M) or M <= 0.0:
        return p, local_score(p)

    K = int(K) if K is not None else 0
    if K > 0:
        K = min(K, c.size)
        idx = np.argpartition(c, -K)[-K:]
        w = np.zeros_like(c, dtype=float)
        w[idx] = 1.0 / float(K)
    else:
        tau = abs(float(tau_frac)) * M
        if tau <= 0.0:
            tau = 1e-12
        z = (c - M) / tau
        expz = np.exp(z)
        s = float(np.sum(expz))
        if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
            w = np.ones_like(c, dtype=float)
            w /= float(c.size)
        else:
            w = expz / s

    y = corr_from_weights(w, p)
    delta = 2.0 * y
    delta = delta - float(np.mean(delta))
    scale = float(np.max(np.abs(delta)))
    if not np.isfinite(scale) or scale <= 0.0:
        return p, local_score(p)
    delta = delta / scale

    best_p = p
    best_sc = local_score(p)
    etas = (0.02, 0.06, 0.15, 0.4, 1.0, 2.5)
    for eta in etas:
        p2 = p * np.exp(-eta * delta)
        p2 = normalize_to_sum1(p2)
        if p2 is None:
            continue
        sc = local_score(p2)
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc:
            best_sc = float(sc)
            best_p = p2
    return best_p, best_sc


def generic_search(p, budget, seed):
    p = normalize_to_sum1(p)
    if p is None:
        return None, float("inf")
    best = p
    best_sc = local_score(p)
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    deadline = t0 + float(max(0.0, budget))
    if deadline - t0 <= 0.0:
        return best, best_sc

    rng = np.random.default_rng(int(seed) + 777)
    tau_opts = np.array([0.005, 0.02, 0.06, 0.2, 0.6], dtype=float)
    k_opts = np.array([4, 16, 64, 256], dtype=int)
    it = 0
    no_improve = 0
    last_lp = 0.0

    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        it += 1
        rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
        if rem < 0.5:
            break

        lp_gap = min(20.0, max(5.0, float(budget) / 10.0))
        if it % 4 == 0 and best.size <= 1024 and (time.monotonic() - last_lp) > lp_gap:
            try:
                cand, sc = try_lp(best, int(seed) + it)
            except Exception:
                cand, sc = None, float("inf")
            last_lp = time.monotonic()
            if cand is not None and np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc:
                best = cand
                best_sc = float(sc)
                no_improve = 0
            continue

        if it % 2 == 0:
            K = int(rng.choice(k_opts))
            tau = 0.0
        else:
            K = 0
            tau = float(rng.choice(tau_opts))

        try:
            cand, sc = generic_multiplicative_update(best, tau, K)
        except Exception:
            cand, sc = None, float("inf")

        if cand is not None and np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc:
            best = cand
            best_sc = float(sc)
            no_improve = 0
        else:
            no_improve += 1

        if no_improve > 22:
            noise = np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, 0.30, best.size))
            cand = normalize_to_sum1(best * noise)
            if cand is not None:
                sc = local_score(cand)
                if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc:
                    best = cand
                    best_sc = float(sc)
            no_improve = 0

    return best, best_sc


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=1500, **kwargs):
    start_time = time.monotonic()
    try:
        budget = float(budget_s)
    except Exception:
        budget = 1500.0
    if not np.isfinite(budget) or budget <= 0.0:
        budget = 1500.0
    hard_deadline = start_time + max(10.0, budget) - 5.0

    best_seq = None
    best_score = float("inf")
    extra_starts = []

    parent_raw = globals().get("height_sequence_1", None)
    parent_arr = None
    if parent_raw is not None:
        p = normalize_to_sum1(parent_raw)
        if p is not None and np.all(np.isfinite(p)):
            parent_arr = p
            best_seq = p.copy()
            best_score = local_score(p)
            print(f"parent length={p.size} score={best_score:.6f}", flush=True)

    if parent_arr is not None and hard_deadline - start_time > 120.0:
        qtime = min(60.0, 0.04 * max(1.0, hard_deadline - start_time))
        if qtime > 5.0:
            try:
                gp, gsc = generic_search(parent_arr, qtime, int(seed) + 1)
            except Exception:
                gp, gsc = None, float("inf")
            if gp is not None and np.isfinite(gsc) and gsc < best_score:
                best_seq = gp.copy()
                best_score = float(gsc)
                print(f"parent generic score={best_score:.6f}", flush=True)

    if budget < 300.0:
        plans = [(512, budget * 0.78)]
    elif budget < 800.0:
        plans = [(512, budget * 0.55), (1024, budget * 0.30)]
    elif budget < 1200.0:
        plans = [(512, budget * 0.50), (1024, budget * 0.32), (2048, budget * 0.13)]
    else:
        plans = [(512, budget * 0.45), (1024, budget * 0.25), (2048, budget * 0.17), (4096, budget * 0.05)]

    if parent_arr is not None:
        pl = int(parent_arr.size)
        if 64 <= pl <= 4096:
            found = False
            for nn, _ in plans:
                if int(nn) == pl:
                    found = True
                    break
            if not found:
                plans.insert(0, (pl, budget * 0.15))

    for n, alloc in plans:
        rem = hard_deadline - time.monotonic()
        if rem < 30.0:
            break
        b = min(float(alloc), rem - 15.0)
        if b < 10.0:
            b = max(5.0, rem * 0.4)
        try:
            q, sc = optimize_anti_length(int(n), b, int(seed) + 1000, parent_arr, extra_starts)
        except Exception as exc:
            print(f"anti length {n} failed: {exc}", flush=True)
            continue
        if q is not None and np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
            best_seq = q.copy()
            best_score = float(sc)
            extra_starts.append(best_seq.copy())
            print(f"anti length={q.size} score={best_score:.6f}", flush=True)
            if best_score < 1.5025:
                break

    rem = hard_deadline - time.monotonic()
    if best_seq is not None and rem > 15.0:
        gen_budget = min(max(30.0, 0.12 * budget), rem - 5.0)
        try:
            gp, gsc = generic_search(best_seq, gen_budget, int(seed) + 999)
        except Exception as exc:
            gp, gsc = None, float("inf")
            print(f"final generic failed: {exc}", flush=True)
        if gp is not None and np.isfinite(gsc) and gsc < best_score:
            best_seq = gp.copy()
            best_score = float(gsc)
            print(f"final generic score={best_score:.6f}", flush=True)

    if best_seq is None:
        if parent_arr is not None:
            best_seq = parent_arr.copy()
            best_score = local_score(best_seq)
        else:
            best_seq = np.ones(64, dtype=float) / 64.0
            best_score = 2.0

    final = normalize_to_sum1(best_seq)
    if final is None:
        final = np.ones(64, dtype=float) / 64.0
    final_list = [float(x) for x in np.clip(np.asarray(final, dtype=float), 0.0, 1000.0)]

    official = best_score
    try:
        official = float(evaluate_sequence(final_list))
    except Exception:
        official = best_score

    if not np.isfinite(official):
        if parent_arr is not None:
            final_list = [float(x) for x in np.clip(np.asarray(parent_arr, dtype=float), 0.0, 1000.0)]
            try:
                official = float(evaluate_sequence(final_list))
            except Exception:
                official = best_score
        else:
            final_list = [1.0 / 64.0 for _ in range(64)]
            official = 2.0

    print(f"FINAL length={len(final_list)} local={best_score:.6f} official={official:.6f}", flush=True)
    return final_list
```
